## INSTRUCTION DESCRIPTIONS

| `rv_y`        | `yaaddu_yy`            | `ym, ys2, ys1, yd`   | `RV-Y 3D adaptation in row-major order (p=(i*COLS+j)*DEPTH+k; COLS is the column extent of the accessed view; DEPTH is the innermost extent). Compute the exact unsigned sum or difference, divide by two with yxrm rounding, and retain the low SEW bits; overflow wraps without setting yxsat. yl and ystart use row-major scalar-element indices. ym controls masking with y0, ys2 selects a source register group, ys1 selects a source register group and yd selects the destination register or group.`                                                                                                                                   |

| `rv_y`        | `ycompress_ym`         | `ys2, ys1, yd`       | `RV-Y 3D adaptation in row-major order (p=(i*COLS+j)*DEPTH+k; COLS is the column extent of the accessed view; DEPTH is the innermost extent). Pack ys2 elements selected by ys1 mask bits into contiguous destination elements starting at zero, preserving source order; remaining elements are tails. ystart must be zero. yl and ystart use row-major scalar-element indices. yd selects the destination register or group.`                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `rv_y`        | `ycpop_m`              | `ym, ys2, rd`        | `RV-Y 3D adaptation in row-major order (p=(i*COLS+j)*DEPTH+k; COLS is the column extent of the accessed view; DEPTH is the innermost extent). | `rv_y`        | `ydivu_yy`             | `ym, ys2, ys1, yd`   | `RV-Y 3D adaptation in row-major order (p=(i*COLS+j)*DEPTH+k; COLS is the column extent of the accessed view; DEPTH is the innermost extent). | `rv_y`        | `ydivu_yx`             | `ym, ys2, rs1, yd`   | `RV-Y 3D adaptation in row-major order (p=(i*COLS+j)*DEPTH+k; COLS is the column extent of the accessed view; DEPTH is the innermost extent). | `rv_y`        | `ydiv_yy`              | `ym, ys2, ys1, yd`   | `RV-Y 3D adaptation in row-major order (p=(i*COLS+j)*DEPTH+k; COLS is the column extent of the accessed view; DEPTH is the innermost extent). | `rv_y`        | `ydiv_yx`              | `ym, ys2, rs1, yd`   | `RV-Y 3D adaptation in row-major order (p=(i*COLS+j)*DEPTH+k; COLS is the column extent of the accessed view; DEPTH is the innermost extent). | `rv_y`        | `yfadd_yf`             | `ym, ys2, rs1, yd`   | `RV-Y 3D adaptation in row-major order (p=(i*COLS+j)*DEPTH+k; COLS is the column extent of the accessed view; DEPTH is the innermost extent).
